    [Role] nvarchar(max) NOT NULL,

    [ResetTokenExpiry] datetime2 NULL,

    [Department] nvarchar(100) NOT NULL,
    [CreatedAt] datetime2 NOT NULL,
    [UpdatedAt] datetime2 NULL,
    CONSTRAINT [PK_AdminProfiles] PRIMARY KEY ([Id])
);
